  .wsec-authors{
    display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr;
    position:relative;z-index:2;
  }
  .wsec-auth{
    grid-row:1;grid-column:1;
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:18px;
    opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px);
    transition:opacity .45s var(--ease), transform .45s var(--ease);
    pointer-events:none;
  }
  .wsec-auth.is-active{opacity:1;transform:none;pointer-events:auto;transition-delay:.22s}
  .wsec-ava{
    width:60px;height:60px;border-radius:50%;overflow:hidden;
    background:var(--bg-2);border:1px solid var(--line);flex-shrink:0;
  }
  .wsec-ava img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;filter:grayscale(.85)}
  .wsec-ava--mono{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;background:var(--accent);border-color:transparent;color:var(--ink);font-weight:600;font-size:20px;letter-spacing:.01em}
  .wsec-aName a{color:var(--ink);transition:color .3s}
  .wsec-aName a:hover{color:var(--accent-deep,#5c8f12)}
  .wsec-aMeta{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;line-height:1.35}
  .wsec-aName{font-size:16px;font-weight:500;color:var(--ink);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;letter-spacing:-.005em}
  .wsec-verif{
    display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    width:18px;height:18px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent);
    box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(198,251,80,.18);
    flex-shrink:0;
  }
  .wsec-verif svg{width:11px;height:11px;color:var(--ink);display:block}
  .wsec-aRole{font-size:13.5px;color:var(--ink-2)}
  .wsec-aTag{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:3px;font-weight:500}
  .wsec-aTag .dot,.wsec-tag .dot{margin:0 6px;color:var(--ink-3);opacity:.7}

  /* Proof rail (RHS) — fixed 380px, perfectly-aligned grid rows */
  .wsec-rail{
    list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0;
    padding-top:120px;
    display:flex;flex-direction:column;
  }
  .wsec-row{
    position:relative;display:grid;
    grid-template-columns:40px 1fr 24px;
    column-gap:20px;align-items:start;
    padding:44px 0;
    border-top:1px solid var(--line);
    cursor:auto;outline:none;
    transition:padding-left .35s var(--ease);
  }
  .wsec-row:last-child{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
  .wsec-row:hover{padding-left:8px}
  .wsec-row:focus-visible{padding-left:8px}
  .wsec-row::before{
    /* Slim lime vertical line on the far left of the active row */
    content:"";position:absolute;left:-12px;top:50%;height:0;width:2px;
    background:var(--accent);border-radius:1px;
    transform:translateY(-50%);
    transition:height .45s var(--ease);
  }
  .wsec-row.is-active::before{height:calc(100% - 48px)}
  .wsec-idx{
    font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;
    font-size:15px;color:var(--ink-3);
    line-height:1.6;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;letter-spacing:-.01em;
    transition:color .35s var(--ease);
  }
  .wsec-row.is-active .wsec-idx{color:#5fa314}
  .wsec-info{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;line-height:1.3;min-width:0}
  .wsec-name{
    font-size:18px;font-weight:500;color:var(--ink-3);letter-spacing:-.015em;line-height:1.25;
    transition:color .35s var(--ease);
  }
  .wsec-row.is-active .wsec-name{color:var(--ink)}
  .wsec-row:hover .wsec-name,
  .wsec-row:focus-visible .wsec-name{color:var(--ink-2)}
  .wsec-row.is-active:hover .wsec-name{color:var(--ink)}
  .wsec-role{font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-2);line-height:1.4}
  .wsec-tag{
    font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;
    color:var(--ink-3);margin-top:4px;font-weight:500;
  }
  .wsec-mark{
    justify-self:end;
    width:9px;height:9px;border-radius:50%;
    background:var(--ink);opacity:.16;
    margin-top:10px;flex-shrink:0;
    transition:background .35s var(--ease), opacity .35s var(--ease), transform .4s cubic-bezier(.34,1.4,.5,1), box-shadow .35s var(--ease);
  }
  .wsec-row.is-active .wsec-mark{
    background:var(--accent);opacity:1;
    box-shadow:0 0 0 5px rgba(198,251,80,.22);
    transform:scale(1.15);
  }
  .wsec-row:hover .wsec-mark{opacity:.55}
  .wsec-row.is-active:hover .wsec-mark{opacity:1}

  /* Verification stamp footer */
  .wsec-foot{
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:18px;
    margin-top:clamp(48px,6vw,76px);padding-top:28px;
    border-top:1px dashed rgba(13,13,13,.14);
    opacity:0;transform:translateY(6px);
    transition:opacity .6s var(--ease) .9s, transform .6s var(--ease) .9s;
  }
  .wsec.is-revealed .wsec-foot{opacity:1;transform:none}
  .wsec-stamp{
    position:relative;width:60px;height:60px;flex-shrink:0;
    display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
  }
  .wsec-stamp .stamp-text{
    position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;color:#5fa314;
    animation:wsecStampSpin 22s linear infinite;
    font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;
  }
  .wsec-stamp .stamp-hand{
    position:absolute;left:28%;top:28%;width:44%;height:44%;
    object-fit:contain;display:block;
  }
  @keyframes wsecStampSpin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
  .wsec-foot-line{
    font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);
    font-weight:500;
  }
  .wsec-foot-line .dotsep{margin:0 10px;color:var(--ink-3);opacity:.6}

  /* Section-enter staggered reveal */
  .wsec .wsec-row{
    opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px);
    transition:opacity .55s var(--ease), transform .55s var(--ease);
    transition-delay:calc(.55s + var(--i,0) * 100ms);
  }
  .wsec.is-revealed .wsec-row{opacity:1;transform:none}
  /* Active mark also animates in after rail rows */
  .wsec .wsec-row.is-active::before{transition-delay:1.1s}

  /* Responsive */
  @media (max-width:1180px){
    .wsec-grid{grid-template-columns:minmax(0,1fr) 340px;gap:64px}
    .wsec-q{font-size:clamp(34px,3.8vw,52px)}
  }
  @media (max-width:980px){
    .wsec-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:56px}
    .wsec-main{padding-top:80px}
    .wsec-qmark{font-size:160px;top:-12px;left:-12px}
    .wsec-rail{
      padding-top:0;
      display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;
    }
    .wsec-row{
      grid-template-columns:32px 1fr 18px;column-gap:14px;
      padding:28px 14px;
      border-top:1px solid var(--line);border-right:1px solid var(--line);
    }
    .wsec-row:nth-child(2n){border-right:0}
    .wsec-row:last-child{border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
    .wsec-row:last-child:nth-child(odd){grid-column:1 / -1}
    .wsec-row::before{left:0;top:auto;bottom:0;height:2px;width:0;transform:none}
    .wsec-row.is-active::before{height:2px;width:60px}
  }
  @media (max-width:640px){
    .wsec-main{padding-top:64px}
    .wsec-q{font-size:26px;line-height:1.2;letter-spacing:-.02em}
    .wsec-qmark{font-size:120px;top:-6px;left:-6px;opacity:.05}
    .wsec-rail{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .wsec-row{border-right:0}
    .wsec-row:last-child:nth-child(odd){grid-column:auto}
    .wsec-foot{gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap}
    .wsec-foot-line{font-size:10.5px}
  }
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
    .wsec-q,.wsec-auth,.wsec-row,.wsec-foot,.wsec-stamp .stamp-text{
      transition:none !important;animation:none !important;transform:none !important;opacity:1 !important;
    }
  }

  /* ─── Stats ─────────────────────────────────────── */
  .stats-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:0;border-top:1px solid var(--line);border-left:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r-card);overflow:hidden}
  .scell{padding:56px 44px;border-right:1px solid var(--line);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);position:relative;overflow:hidden;background:var(--bg);transition:background .4s}
  .scell:hover{background:var(--bg-2)}
  .scell .big{font-size:clamp(80px,10vw,140px);font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.05em;line-height:.9;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
  .scell .big sup{font-size:.4em;vertical-align:super;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:400}
  .scell .blabel{font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-2);margin-top:14px;max-width:300px;line-height:1.5}
  .scell .stag{position:absolute;top:24px;right:28px;font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px}
  .scell .stag::before{content:"";width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent)}

  /* ─── FAQ (chat conversation) ───────────────────── */
  .chat{position:relative;padding:8px 0 0}
  .chat::before,.chat::after{
    content:"";position:absolute;top:0;bottom:0;width:1px;
    background-image:linear-gradient(to bottom, var(--line-2) 0 6px, transparent 6px 14px);
    background-size:1px 14px;background-repeat:repeat-y;pointer-events:none;
  }
  .chat::before{left:6px} .chat::after{right:6px}

  .chat-feed{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:18px;position:relative}

  /* Generic row */
  .msg{display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:14px;max-width:78%;position:relative}
  .msg.from-you{align-self:flex-end;flex-direction:row-reverse}
  .msg.from-bot{align-self:flex-start}

  /* Avatar (hand logomark) for the bot, only shown above the active answer */
  .ans-row > .ava{
    width:56px;height:56px;min-width:56px;min-height:56px;
    border-radius:50%;flex-shrink:0;flex-grow:0;
    background:var(--accent);
    box-shadow:0 10px 24px -12px rgba(92,143,18,.5);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    overflow:hidden;
    margin-right:16px;position:relative;z-index:2;
    align-self:flex-end;
  }
  .ans-row > .ava img{
    width:58%;height:58%;object-fit:contain;
    pointer-events:none;display:block;
  }
  .msg .ava.placeholder{visibility:hidden;width:0;height:0;margin-right:-14px}

  /* User question pill (right side, dark) */
  .q-btn{
    appearance:none;border:0;background:transparent;cursor:auto;
    display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;
    font:inherit;color:inherit;padding:0;
    transition:transform .4s var(--ease);
  }
  .q-btn:hover{transform:translateX(-4px)}
  .q-btn .q-toggle{
    width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:50%;flex-shrink:0;
    border:1px solid var(--line);background:transparent;color:var(--ink-2);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    transition:transform .55s var(--ease),background .35s,border-color .35s,color .35s;
    font-size:18px;line-height:1;font-weight:300;
  }
  .q-btn .q-toggle svg{width:14px;height:14px}
  .q-btn .q-toggle .ic-plus,.q-btn .q-toggle .ic-min{position:absolute;transition:opacity .25s var(--ease),transform .35s var(--ease)}
  .q-bubble{
    padding:16px 24px;border-radius:24px 24px 6px 24px;
    background:var(--bg-2);color:var(--ink);
    font-size:16px;line-height:1.4;letter-spacing:-.005em;
    max-width:520px;transition:background .35s var(--ease),color .35s var(--ease);
    box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--line);
  }

  /* Active state on the question button */
  .msg.active .q-btn .q-toggle{
    background:var(--ink);border-color:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);transform:rotate(180deg);
  }
  .msg.active .q-bubble{
    background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);
    border-radius:24px 24px 6px 24px;box-shadow:0 18px 40px -22px rgba(0,0,0,.45);
  }
  .msg.active .q-btn{transform:translateX(0)}

  /* Bot answer card (left side, cream/white) */
  .ans-row{
    display:none;align-self:flex-start;max-width:86%;
    gap:14px;align-items:flex-end;
    animation:bot-in .6s var(--ease) both;
  }
  .msg.active + .ans-row{display:flex}
  @keyframes bot-in{
    0%{opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px)}
    100%{opacity:1;transform:none}
  }
  .ans-bubble{
    position:relative;
    padding:22px 26px 22px 26px;border-radius:6px 24px 24px 24px;
    background:#fff;color:var(--ink);
    font-size:15.5px;line-height:1.6;letter-spacing:-.003em;
    max-width:560px;
    box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--line),0 24px 50px -28px rgba(0,0,0,.16);
  }
  .ans-bubble .ans-meta{
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;
    margin-top:14px;padding-top:14px;border-top:1px dashed var(--line);
    font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);
  }
  .ans-bubble .ans-meta .pulse{
    width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent);
    box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(198,251,80,.45);animation:livepulse 2.4s ease-out infinite;
  }
  .ans-bubble .ans-meta .by{color:var(--ink-2);font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:14px;letter-spacing:0;text-transform:none}

  /* Typing indicator */
  .typing{
    display:none;align-items:center;gap:6px;
    padding:18px 22px;border-radius:6px 24px 24px 24px;background:#fff;
    box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--line);
  }
  .typing span{
    width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--ink-3);
    animation:typdot 1.1s ease-in-out infinite;
  }
  .typing span:nth-child(2){animation-delay:.15s}
  .typing span:nth-child(3){animation-delay:.3s}
  @keyframes typdot{
    0%,60%,100%{transform:translateY(0);opacity:.5}
    30%{transform:translateY(-5px);opacity:1}
  }
  .msg.thinking .q-bubble + .ans-row,
  .msg.active.is-typing + .ans-row .ans-bubble{display:none}
  .msg.active.is-typing + .ans-row .typing{display:flex}
  .msg.active.is-typing + .ans-row .ava{background-color:var(--accent-deep,#5c8f12)}

  /* Conversation header (status bar above feed) */
  .chat-status{
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;margin-bottom:36px;
    padding:14px 22px;border-radius:99px;background:#fff;width:fit-content;
    box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--line);
  }
  .chat-status .ava-sm{
    width:36px;height:36px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--ink) url('assets/logo-mark.svg') center/58% no-repeat;
    position:relative;
  }
  .chat-status .ava-sm::after{
    content:"";position:absolute;right:-1px;bottom:-1px;width:11px;height:11px;border-radius:50%;
    background:var(--accent);border:2px solid #fff;
  }
  .chat-status .who{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:2px;line-height:1.1}
  .chat-status .who b{font-weight:500;font-size:14px}
  .chat-status .who span{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.12em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
  .chat-status .who span em{font-style:normal;color:var(--accent);font-weight:500}

  /* Composer card at end of feed */
  .chat-compose{
    margin-top:36px;padding:22px 24px;border-radius:24px;
    background:#fff;box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--line);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:24px;
    transition:transform .4s var(--ease),box-shadow .4s var(--ease);
  }
  .chat-compose:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:inset 0 0 0 1px var(--ink),0 20px 40px -20px rgba(0,0,0,.18)}
  .chat-compose .cc-prompt{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;flex:1;min-width:0}
  .chat-compose .cc-ava{width:36px;height:36px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent-soft);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;color:var(--ink);flex-shrink:0}
  .chat-compose .cc-ava svg{width:18px;height:18px}
  .chat-compose .cc-text{font-size:15px;color:var(--ink-2);font-feature-settings:"ss01"}
  .chat-compose .cc-text em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;color:var(--ink)}
  .chat-compose .cc-send{
    display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;
    padding:12px 18px;border-radius:99px;background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);
    font-size:13px;letter-spacing:.04em;text-decoration:none;
    transition:transform .35s var(--ease),background .3s;
  }
  .chat-compose .cc-send:hover{background:var(--accent);transform:translateX(3px)}
  .chat-compose .cc-send svg{width:14px;height:14px}

  @media (max-width:720px){
    .msg{max-width:92%}
    .q-bubble,.ans-bubble{font-size:14px;padding:14px 18px}
    .chat::before,.chat::after{display:none}
  }

  
  /* FAQ block inside Contact */
  .faq-block{margin-top:120px;padding-top:80px;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}
  .faq-block-h{font-size:clamp(40px,5vw,72px);font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.03em;line-height:1.05;color:var(--ink);margin:0 0 56px;text-wrap:balance;max-width:18ch}
  .faq-block-h em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink-2)}

  /* ─── Contact ───────────────────────────────────── */
  .contact-inner{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:48px;align-items:start}
  .contact h2{font-size:clamp(48px,7vw,120px);font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.04em;line-height:.95}
  .contact h2 em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink-2)}
  .contact .clist{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:0;border-top:1px solid var(--line);margin-top:40px}
  .contact .clist a{
    display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;
    padding:20px 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);font-size:18px;
    transition:padding .4s var(--ease);position:relative;cursor:auto;
  }
  .contact .clist a:hover{padding-left:14px}
  .contact .clist a::after{
    content:"↗";position:absolute;right:0;color:var(--ink-3);transition:transform .4s var(--ease),color .3s;font-size:18px;
  }
  .contact .clist a:hover::after{transform:translate(4px,-4px);color:var(--ink)}
  .contact .clist a span{color:var(--ink-2);font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.15em;text-transform:uppercase}

  .form{background:var(--bg);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:var(--r-card);padding:36px}
  .form .row{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;margin-bottom:24px}
  .form label{font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.15em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-2)}
  .form input,.form textarea{
    background:transparent;border:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
    padding:10px 0;font-family:inherit;font-size:16px;color:var(--ink);outline:none;
    transition:border-color .3s;
  }
  .form input:focus,.form textarea:focus{border-color:var(--ink)}
  .form textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:90px}
  .form button{
    width:100%;background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);border:0;padding:18px;border-radius:var(--r-pill);
    font-family:inherit;font-size:15px;font-weight:500;cursor:auto;margin-top:8px;
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:10px;
    position:relative;overflow:hidden;
    transition:transform .35s var(--ease), box-shadow .4s var(--ease);
  }
  /* Hero-button style: radial accent glow on hover */
  .form button::before{
    content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;
    background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(198,251,80,.55), transparent 65%);
    opacity:0;transition:opacity .4s var(--ease);pointer-events:none;
  }
  .form button:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 24px 60px -20px rgba(15,15,14,.7)}
  .form button:hover::before{opacity:1}
  .form button > *{position:relative;z-index:1}
  .form button .arr{
    display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;color:var(--ink);
    font-size:13px;line-height:1;
    transition:transform .35s var(--ease), background .35s var(--ease);
  }
  .form button:hover .arr{transform:translate(2px,-2px) rotate(-6deg);background:var(--accent)}

  .pricing-toggle{
    display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:0;margin-top:6px;
    background:var(--accent);padding:4px;border-radius:var(--r-pill);position:relative;
  }
  .pricing-toggle button{
    background:transparent;border:0;color:rgba(13,13,13,.55);padding:0 16px;border-radius:var(--r-pill);
    font-family:inherit;font-size:13px;cursor:auto;letter-spacing:.02em;font-weight:500;
    transition:color .35s;position:relative;z-index:1;
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;line-height:1;min-height:46px;
  }
  .pricing-toggle button.is-on{color:var(--ink)}
  /* Toggle buttons must NOT inherit the .form button hover glow / lift / shadow */
  .pricing-toggle button::before{display:none !important}
  .pricing-toggle button:hover{transform:none;box-shadow:none}
  .pricing-toggle .slider{
    position:absolute;top:4px;bottom:4px;width:calc(50% - 4px);
    background:#fff;border-radius:var(--r-pill);
    box-shadow:0 1px 3px rgba(0,0,0,.08);
    transition:transform .45s var(--ease);left:4px;
  }
  .pricing-toggle.right .slider{transform:translateX(100%)}
  .form-status{margin-top:14px;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-2);min-height:1em}

  /* ─── Footer ────────────────────────────────────── */
  .ft-marquee{background:var(--bg);border-top:1px solid var(--line);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);width:100%}
  .footer-mq{overflow:hidden;padding:0;background:transparent;border:0}
  .footer-mq .track{display:flex;gap:0;white-space:nowrap;align-items:center;font-size:clamp(56px,6.8vw,91px);font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.045em;line-height:1;padding:48px 0 28px;animation:scroll-x 70s linear infinite;width:max-content}
  .footer-mq .track .phrase{display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:center;padding-right:.5em;line-height:.95}
  .footer-mq .track .phrase em{display:block;font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-style:normal;font-weight:500;font-size:.16em;letter-spacing:.04em;color:var(--ink-2);padding:.4em 0 0;line-height:1.1;text-transform:none}
  .footer-mq .track .sep{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;margin:0 .35em;color:var(--accent);flex-shrink:0}
  .footer-mq .track .sep svg{width:.55em;height:.55em;display:block;animation:spin 14s linear infinite}
  @keyframes spin{from{transform:rotate(0deg)}to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
  

  /* ─── Footer (dark, editorial) ─── */
  .footer-bot{display:none}
  footer{background:var(--ink);color:#f4f4f4;position:relative;overflow:hidden;padding:clamp(80px,8vw,120px) clamp(40px,5vw,100px) 40px}
  .ft-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1.1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:80px;margin-bottom:64px;position:relative;z-index:2}
  .ft-col{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:18px}
  .ft-eyebrow{font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:rgba(244,244,244,.45);font-weight:500;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
  .ft-eyebrow .dot{width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent);box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(198,251,80,.5);animation:livepulse 2.4s ease-out infinite}
  .ft-studio h3{font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-weight:500;font-size:clamp(32px,3.2vw,44px);letter-spacing:-.025em;line-height:1.05;color:#fff;margin:0}
  .ft-studio h3 em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:rgba(244,244,244,.55)}
  .ft-time{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;margin-top:8px}
  .ft-time span{font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:rgba(244,244,244,.45);font-weight:500}
  .ft-time b{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:30px;font-weight:400;letter-spacing:-.01em;font-feature-settings:"tnum";color:#fff;line-height:1}

  .ft-nav ul,.ft-social ul{list-style:none;padding:0;margin:0;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px}
  .ft-nav a,.ft-social a{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-size:18px;color:#f4f4f4;text-decoration:none;transition:transform .35s var(--ease),color .3s}
  .ft-nav a:hover,.ft-social a:hover{transform:translateX(6px);color:var(--accent)}
  .ft-nav a .num,.ft-social a .arr{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:13px;color:rgba(244,244,244,.4);font-weight:400;transition:color .3s}
  .ft-nav a:hover .num,.ft-social a:hover .arr{color:var(--accent)}

  /* Giant wordmark — inverted, gradient-faded into footer floor */
  .ft-mark{display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;align-items:flex-end;padding:24px 0 0;position:relative;z-index:1;cursor:default;margin:0 -40px 0 0;overflow:hidden}
  .ft-mark img{width:min(62%,980px);height:auto;display:block;user-select:none;pointer-events:none;transition:transform 1.2s var(--ease)}
  .ft-mark::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;height:55%;pointer-events:none;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(13,13,13,0) 0%,rgba(13,13,13,.55) 35%,rgba(13,13,13,1) 90%)}
  footer:hover .ft-mark img{transform:translateY(-6px)}

  .ft-bot{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:24px 48px;padding:24px 0 0;border-top:1px solid rgba(244,244,244,.08);position:relative;z-index:2;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:rgba(244,244,244,.45)}
  .ft-bot a{color:rgba(244,244,244,.45);text-decoration:none;transition:color .3s}
  .ft-bot a:hover{color:var(--accent)}

  /* ─── D&B D-U-N-S Registered trust seal ─── */
  .ft-verify{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:22px;flex-wrap:wrap;padding:28px 0;margin-top:8px;border-top:1px solid rgba(244,244,244,.08);position:relative;z-index:2}
  /* Slot holding the official D&B D-U-N-S Registered seal iframe */
  .dnb-seal-slot{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-width:104px;line-height:0}
  .dnb-seal-slot img{display:block;height:auto;max-width:160px}
  .dnb-seal-slot iframe{display:block;width:160px;height:130px;border:0}
  /* Styled fallback badge — shows in preview & on any unregistered domain */
  .dnb-badge{display:inline-flex;align-items:stretch;gap:0;border:1px solid rgba(244,244,244,.16);border-radius:8px;overflow:hidden;background:rgba(244,244,244,.025);text-decoration:none;transition:border-color .35s,background .35s}
  .dnb-badge:hover{border-color:rgba(244,244,244,.34);background:rgba(244,244,244,.05)}
  .dnb-badge .db-mark{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:2px;padding:12px 16px;background:var(--accent);color:#0d0d0d;flex-shrink:0}
  .dnb-badge .db-mark b{font-size:21px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-.03em;line-height:1;font-family:"Onest",sans-serif}
  .dnb-badge .db-mark span{font-size:7.5px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;line-height:1}
  .dnb-badge .db-body{display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;gap:9px;padding:13px 18px}
  .dnb-badge .db-body .db-t{font-size:12px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.02em;color:#f4f4f4;line-height:1.15}
  .dnb-badge .db-body .db-t sup{font-size:.62em;top:-.5em}
  .dnb-badge .db-body .db-n{font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.2em;text-transform:uppercase;color:rgba(244,244,244,.5);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}
  .ft-verify .db-note{font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;color:rgba(244,244,244,.34);max-width:240px;line-height:1.6}

  @media (max-width:1280px){
    footer{padding:100px 64px 32px}
    .ft-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:56px}
    .ft-social{grid-column:1/-1}
    .ft-social ul{flex-direction:row;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:24px}
    .ft-mark{margin:0 -64px}
  }
  @media (max-width:680px){
    footer{padding:64px 28px 28px}
    .ft-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:40px;margin-bottom:40px}
    .ft-mark{margin:0 -28px 0 0}
    .ft-mark img{width:min(78%,520px)}
    .ft-social ul,.ft-nav ul{flex-direction:column;gap:10px}
    .ft-bot{flex-direction:column;gap:14px;text-align:left}
  }

    /* Blog */
  .blog-list{display:flex;flex-direction:column;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}
  .blog-row{display:grid;grid-template-columns:auto 1fr auto;gap:32px;align-items:start;padding:32px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);position:relative;color:var(--ink);transition:padding-left .5s var(--ease),background .5s var(--ease)}
  .blog-row:hover{padding-left:24px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,rgba(0,0,0,.04),transparent 60%)}
  .blog-row .bl-meta{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);min-width:140px}
  .blog-row h3{font-size:26px;font-weight:500;line-height:1.15;letter-spacing:-.02em;margin:0 0 8px;text-wrap:balance}
  .blog-row p{font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-2);line-height:1.55;max-width:520px;text-wrap:pretty}
  .blog-row .bl-arrow{font-size:22px;color:var(--ink-3);transition:transform .5s var(--ease),color .5s var(--ease);align-self:center}
  .blog-row:hover .bl-arrow{transform:translateX(8px) rotate(-45deg);color:var(--accent)}
  @media (max-width:720px){.blog-row{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:12px}.blog-row .bl-arrow{justify-self:end}}

  /* ─── Side dots ─────────────────────────────────── */
  .side-dots{position:fixed;right:24px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);z-index:150;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:14px;align-items:flex-end}
  .side-dots a{
    width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:99px;background:var(--ink-3);transition:all .35s var(--ease);
    display:block;position:relative;
  }
  .side-dots a.is-active{background:var(--ink);width:18px}
  .side-dots a::before{
    content:attr(data-l);position:absolute;right:14px;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);
    font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.15em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-2);
    opacity:0;white-space:nowrap;transition:opacity .25s;background:var(--bg);padding:3px 8px;border-radius:99px;border:1px solid var(--line);
  }
  .side-dots a:hover::before{opacity:1}

  /* ─── Curtain ───────────────────────────────────── */
  .curtain{position:fixed;inset:0;background:var(--ink);z-index:9500;transform-origin:top;transition:transform 1.1s cubic-bezier(.7,.05,.2,1) .2s;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center}
  .curtain.gone{transform:scaleY(0)}
  .curtain{background:#fcfcfc}
  .curtain .cl-meta,.curtain .cl-bot{color:#0d0d0d;opacity:.55}
  .curtain .cl-bot .pct{color:#0d0d0d}
  .curtain .cl-mark{
    width:30vw;max-width:380px;
    transform:scale(.92);opacity:0;transition:opacity .8s var(--ease),transform .9s var(--ease);
    filter:none;
  }
  .curtain .cl-mark img{width:100%;display:block}
  .curtain.show .cl-mark{opacity:1;transform:scale(1)}
  .curtain.gone .cl-mark{opacity:0;transition:opacity .3s}
  .curtain .cl-meta{position:absolute;left:32px;right:32px;top:32px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--bg);opacity:.55}
  .curtain .cl-bot{position:absolute;left:32px;right:32px;bottom:32px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--bg);opacity:.55}
  .curtain .cl-bot .pct{color:var(--bg);opacity:1;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;font-weight:500}

  @media (max-width:1100px){
    .work .preview{display:none}
    .work{grid-template-columns:60px 1fr 180px 80px 36px}
  }
  @media (max-width:1280px){
    .split{grid-template-columns:440px 1fr}
    .split-aside{padding:120px 60px 60px}
    .rsec{padding:120px 100px}
  }
  @media (max-width:960px){
    .split{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .split-aside{position:relative;height:auto;padding:64px 24px;border-right:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
    .aside-stack{height:auto;min-height:280px}
    .rsec{padding:64px 24px}
    .work{grid-template-columns:40px 1fr 80px 36px}
    .work .meta{display:none}
    .work .title{font-size:28px}
    .srv{grid-template-columns:40px 1fr 40px}
    .srv .desc{display:none}
    .srv .name{font-size:24px}
    .stats-grid,.process-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .scell .big{font-size:88px}
    .why-card{grid-template-columns:1fr;padding:32px;position:relative;top:auto}
    .benefits-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .contact-inner{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .footer-bot{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .hero-meta{position:relative;top:auto;left:auto;right:auto;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:18px;margin-bottom:24px}
    .hero{padding-top:96px}
    .hero-bottom{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:24px}
    .nav-time{display:none}
  }

  /* ─── Thread Nav (creative navigation) ─────────── */
  .thread-host{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:300;height:130px;pointer-events:none;user-select:none}
  .thread-logo{position:absolute;top:30px;left:100px;z-index:6;pointer-events:auto;display:flex;align-items:center;transition:transform .55s var(--ease)}
  .thread-logo:hover{transform:scale(1.04)}
  .thread-logo img{height:46px;width:auto;display:block;transition:opacity .4s var(--ease)}
  .thread-logo .tl-dark{position:absolute;top:0;left:0;opacity:0}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-logo .tl-light{opacity:0}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-logo .tl-dark{opacity:1}
  .thread-host::before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(255,255,255,1) 0%,rgba(255,255,255,.98) 40%,rgba(255,255,255,.85) 70%,rgba(255,255,255,0) 100%);backdrop-filter:blur(10px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:blur(10px);pointer-events:none;mask:linear-gradient(180deg,#000 0%,#000 70%,transparent 100%)}
  .thread-svg{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;display:block;pointer-events:none;overflow:visible}
  .thread-svg .wire{fill:none;stroke:var(--ink);stroke-width:1.1;stroke-linecap:round;opacity:.85}
  .thread-svg .wire-prog{fill:none;stroke:#A2E019;stroke-width:1.6;stroke-linecap:round}
  .thread-svg .peg{fill:rgba(34,33,30,.22);transition:fill .35s var(--ease)}
  .thread-svg .peg.is-active{fill:#A2E019;r:4}
  .thread-svg .peg.is-passed{fill:var(--ink)}
  .thread-labels{position:absolute;inset:0;pointer-events:none}
  .thread-label{position:absolute;top:62%;transform:translate(-50%,-100%);pointer-events:auto;cursor:pointer;padding:10px 6px 14px;text-align:center;color:var(--ink-2);font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:21px;line-height:1;letter-spacing:-.01em;transition:color .35s var(--ease),transform .55s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1),opacity .35s var(--ease);white-space:nowrap;text-decoration:none}
  .thread-label .lbl-num{display:block;font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-style:normal;font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-bottom:5px;transition:color .35s var(--ease)}
  .thread-label:hover{color:var(--ink)}
  .thread-label.is-active{color:var(--ink);font-weight:500}
  .thread-label.is-active .lbl-num{color:var(--ink);font-weight:600}
  .thread-label.is-vibing{animation:vibe .6s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)}
  @keyframes vibe{0%{transform:translate(-50%,-100%)}25%{transform:translate(calc(-50% + 3px),calc(-100% - 6px))}50%{transform:translate(calc(-50% - 2px),calc(-100% - 3px))}75%{transform:translate(calc(-50% + 1px),calc(-100% - 5px))}100%{transform:translate(-50%,-100%)}}
  .thread-label::after{content:"";position:absolute;left:50%;bottom:-2px;transform:translateX(-50%);width:1px;height:10px;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(34,33,30,0),rgba(34,33,30,.35));transition:height .35s var(--ease)}
  .thread-label.is-active::after{height:18px;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(34,33,30,0),var(--ink))}
  .thread-hand{position:absolute;top:0;left:0;width:60px;height:60px;pointer-events:auto;cursor:grab;will-change:transform;transform:translate(-50%,0)}
  .thread-hand:active,.thread-hand.is-dragging{cursor:grabbing}
  .thread-hand .pin{position:absolute;top:-2px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--ink);box-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(34,33,30,.3)}
  .thread-hand .pendant{position:absolute;top:50%;left:50%;transform:translate(-50%,-50%);width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--ink);border:1px solid var(--ink);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;box-shadow:0 10px 24px -6px rgba(0,0,0,.25),0 0 0 4px rgba(245,243,236,.6);transition:transform .55s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1),background .35s var(--ease),border-color .35s var(--ease)}
  .thread-hand .pendant svg{width:22px;height:22px;color:var(--bg);transition:color .35s var(--ease)}
  /* Per-section icon swap inside the pendant */
  .thread-hand .pendant .pi{position:absolute;top:50%;left:50%;width:20px;height:20px;color:var(--bg);opacity:0;transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(.65) rotate(-8deg);transition:opacity .32s var(--ease),transform .5s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)}
  .thread-hand .pendant[data-active="works"] .pi-works,
  .thread-hand .pendant[data-active="services"] .pi-services,
  .thread-hand .pendant[data-active="process"] .pi-process,
  .thread-hand .pendant[data-active="why"] .pi-why,
  .thread-hand .pendant[data-active="words"] .pi-words,
  .thread-hand .pendant[data-active="contact"] .pi-contact{opacity:1;transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1) rotate(0)}
  .thread-hand:hover .pendant{transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1.06)}
  .thread-hand.is-dragging .pendant{background:var(--ink);border-color:var(--ink);transform:translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1.1)}
  /* Hand tooltip */
  .thread-hand::after{
    content:"Drag to scrub";
    position:absolute;left:50%;top:calc(50% + 30px);
    transform:translate(-50%,4px);
    padding:5px 10px;border-radius:99px;
    background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);
    font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-size:9px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;
    white-space:nowrap;pointer-events:none;
    opacity:0;transition:opacity .25s var(--ease),transform .25s var(--ease);
  }
  .thread-hand:hover::after,.thread-hand:focus-visible::after,.thread-hand.is-dragging::after{opacity:1;transform:translate(-50%,0)}
  /* First-load nudge — auto-shows the tooltip once */
  body.is-loaded .thread-hand::after{opacity:1;transform:translate(-50%,0);animation:tooltipNudge 4s var(--ease) 1.2s forwards}
  @keyframes tooltipNudge{0%,75%{opacity:1;transform:translate(-50%,0)}100%{opacity:0;transform:translate(-50%,4px)}}
  body.is-loaded .thread-hand:hover::after,
  body.is-loaded .thread-hand.is-dragging::after{opacity:1 !important;transform:translate(-50%,0) !important;animation:none !important}
  @media (max-width:760px){.thread-host{height:80px}.thread-label{font-size:14px}.thread-label .lbl-num{display:none}.thread-hand{width:46px;height:46px}.thread-hand .pendant{width:36px;height:36px}.thread-hand .pendant svg{width:18px;height:18px}.thread-hint{display:none}}

  /* Top nav adapts to dark backgrounds (footer) */
  .thread-host.on-dark::before{background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(13,13,13,.95) 0%,rgba(13,13,13,.6) 60%,rgba(13,13,13,0) 100%)}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-label{color:rgba(255,255,255,.45)}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-label .lbl-num{color:rgba(255,255,255,.35)}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-label:hover{color:#fff}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-label.is-active{color:#fff}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-label.is-active .lbl-num{color:#fff}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-label::after{background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(255,255,255,0),rgba(255,255,255,.35))}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-label.is-active::after{background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(255,255,255,0),#fff)}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-svg .wire{stroke:rgba(255,255,255,.5)}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-svg .wire-prog{stroke:#A2E019}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-svg .peg{fill:rgba(255,255,255,.4)}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-svg .peg.is-active{fill:#A2E019}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-svg .peg.is-passed{fill:#fff}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-hand .pin{background:#fff;box-shadow:0 0 8px rgba(255,255,255,.4)}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-hand .pendant{background:#fff;border-color:#fff}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-hand .pendant svg{color:var(--ink)}
  .thread-host.on-dark .thread-logo img{filter:none}

  .bot-nav{display:none !important}

  /* ─── Case Study Overlay ──────────────────────── */
  .case-study{
    position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9800;background:var(--bg);
    transform:translateY(100%);transition:transform .85s cubic-bezier(.7,.05,.2,1);
    overflow-y:auto;overflow-x:hidden;
    -webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;
    visibility:hidden;
  }
  .case-study.is-open{transform:translateY(0);visibility:visible}
  body.cs-locked{overflow:hidden}

  /* Scroll progress rail */
  .cs-rail{position:fixed;top:0;left:0;bottom:0;width:2px;background:rgba(13,13,13,.06);z-index:9900;pointer-events:none}
  .cs-rail i{position:absolute;top:0;left:0;right:0;height:0;background:var(--accent);transition:height .05s linear}

  /* Top chrome */
  .cs-top{
    position:sticky;top:0;z-index:5;background:rgba(255,255,255,.85);backdrop-filter:saturate(1.4) blur(14px);-webkit-backdrop-filter:saturate(1.4) blur(14px);
    display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto 1fr;align-items:center;gap:24px;
    padding:18px 48px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
  }
  .cs-back{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink);background:transparent;border:0;cursor:auto;font-family:inherit;padding:0;transition:transform .35s var(--ease)}
  .cs-back:hover{transform:translateX(-4px)}
  .cs-back .arr{width:32px;height:32px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:50%;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;transition:background .3s,color .3s,border-color .3s}
  .cs-back:hover .arr{background:var(--ink);color:var(--bg);border-color:var(--ink)}
  .cs-back .arr svg{width:14px;height:14px}
  .cs-title{justify-self:center;font-size:13px;letter-spacing:.04em;color:var(--ink-2);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
  .cs-title b{color:var(--ink);font-weight:500}
  .cs-title em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink-3);font-size:14px}
  .cs-index{justify-self:end;font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:18px;color:var(--ink-3);font-feature-settings:"tnum"}
  .cs-index b{color:var(--ink);font-weight:400}

  /* Hero */
  .cs-hero{
    position:relative;height:88vh;min-height:560px;overflow:hidden;background:var(--bg-2);
  }
  .cs-hero img{position:absolute;inset:0;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;transform:scale(1.04);transition:transform 1.5s var(--ease)}
  .case-study.is-open .cs-hero img{transform:scale(1)}
  .cs-hero::after{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,rgba(0,0,0,0) 40%,rgba(0,0,0,.55) 100%);pointer-events:none}
  .cs-hero-text{position:absolute;left:48px;right:48px;bottom:48px;z-index:1;color:#fff;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:18px;max-width:1100px}
  .cs-hero-text .industry-pill{
    align-self:flex-start;font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;
    padding:9px 16px;background:rgba(198,251,80,.95);color:var(--ink);border-radius:99px;font-weight:500;
  }
  .cs-hero-text h2{font-size:clamp(54px,7vw,112px);font-weight:500;line-height:1;letter-spacing:-.035em;text-wrap:balance}
  .cs-hero-text h2 em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400}
  .cs-hero-text .kicker{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:clamp(22px,2vw,28px);max-width:740px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.92);line-height:1.3}
  .cs-hero .cs-live{
    position:absolute;top:48px;right:48px;z-index:1;
    display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:11px 18px;border-radius:99px;
    background:rgba(255,255,255,.94);color:var(--ink);font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;font-weight:500;
    text-decoration:none;transition:background .3s,color .3s;
  }
  .cs-hero .cs-live{display:none !important}

  /* At-a-glance strip — now hosts the View Live CTA at the end */
  .cs-meta{
    display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:flex-start;
    column-gap:clamp(40px,5vw,84px);row-gap:28px;
    padding:48px clamp(40px,5vw,100px);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);background:#fafafa;
  }
  .cs-meta .cs-live{
    position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;
    margin-left:auto;align-self:center;
    padding:14px 14px 14px 22px;border-radius:99px;
    background:var(--ink);color:#fff;
    font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.16em;text-transform:uppercase;
    text-decoration:none;white-space:nowrap;justify-self:end;
    box-shadow:0 12px 24px -10px rgba(0,0,0,.35);
    transition:transform .45s var(--ease),box-shadow .45s var(--ease);
    overflow:hidden;
  }
  .cs-meta .cs-live::before{
    content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;
    background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(198,251,80,.55), transparent 65%);
    opacity:0;transition:opacity .4s var(--ease);pointer-events:none;
  }
  .cs-meta .cs-live:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 24px 48px -18px rgba(15,15,14,.7)}
  .cs-meta .cs-live:hover::before{opacity:1}
  .cs-meta .cs-live > span{position:relative;z-index:1}
  .cs-meta .cs-live .arrow{
    position:relative;z-index:1;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;
    background:#fff;color:var(--ink);display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    transition:transform .45s var(--ease),background .35s var(--ease);
    flex-shrink:0;
  }
  .cs-meta .cs-live:hover .arrow{transform:translate(2px,-2px) rotate(-6deg);background:var(--accent)}
  .cs-meta .cs-live .arrow svg{width:11px;height:11px;display:block;transition:none}
  .cs-meta .cs-live svg{width:14px;height:14px}
  .cs-meta .cs-live:hover svg{transform:none}
  /* Actions: website "View site" pill or App Store / Play Store badges */
  .cs-meta .cs-actions{margin-left:auto;align-self:center;display:flex;gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center}
  .cs-meta .cs-actions .cs-live{margin-left:0}
  .cs-store{
    position:relative;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:11px;
    padding:11px 22px 11px 18px;border-radius:99px;
    background:var(--ink);color:#fff;text-decoration:none;white-space:nowrap;
    box-shadow:0 12px 24px -10px rgba(0,0,0,.35);
    transition:transform .45s var(--ease),box-shadow .45s var(--ease);
    overflow:hidden;
  }
  .cs-store::before{content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;
    background:radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%,rgba(198,251,80,.55),transparent 65%);
    opacity:0;transition:opacity .4s var(--ease);pointer-events:none}
  .cs-store:hover{transform:translateY(-2px);box-shadow:0 24px 48px -18px rgba(15,15,14,.7)}
  .cs-store:hover::before{opacity:1}
  .cs-store svg{position:relative;z-index:1;height:23px;width:auto;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:-1px}
  .cs-store .lbl{position:relative;z-index:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;line-height:1.05;text-align:left}
  .cs-store .lbl small{font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-size:8.5px;letter-spacing:.13em;text-transform:uppercase;opacity:.72;font-weight:500}
  .cs-store .lbl b{font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;letter-spacing:-.01em}
  .cs-meta .col{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:6px;flex:0 0 auto;max-width:260px}
  .cs-meta .col b{font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:500}
  .cs-meta .col span{font-size:15px;color:var(--ink);font-weight:450;font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-style:normal;letter-spacing:-.005em;line-height:1.4}

  /* Content sections */
  .cs-sec{padding:clamp(96px,10vw,140px) clamp(40px,10vw,200px);border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);position:relative}
  .cs-sec:last-of-type{border-bottom:0}
  .cs-eyebrow{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.24em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-2);margin-bottom:56px;font-weight:500}
  .cs-eyebrow .num{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;width:34px;height:34px;min-width:34px;min-height:34px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent);color:var(--ink);font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-style:normal;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.04em;font-weight:600;font-feature-settings:"tnum";box-shadow:0 0 0 4px rgba(198,251,80,.18);transition:transform .5s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1),box-shadow .35s var(--ease)}
  .cs-sec:hover .cs-eyebrow .num,.cs-ai:hover .cs-eyebrow .num,.cs-related:hover .cs-eyebrow .num{transform:scale(1.08);box-shadow:0 0 0 6px rgba(198,251,80,.28)}
  .cs-eyebrow .line{flex:1;width:auto;min-width:60px;height:1px;background:var(--line)}
  .cs-sec .cs-eyebrow .line{width:60px;height:1px;background:var(--line)}

  /* Intro paragraph */
  .cs-intro p{font-size:clamp(22px,2.1vw,30px);line-height:1.45;letter-spacing:-.015em;color:var(--ink);max-width:920px;font-weight:400;text-wrap:pretty}
  .cs-intro p em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink)}

  /* Challenge / Solution / Outcome 3-up */
  .cs-cso{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:32px}
  .cs-cso .card{
    background:#fafafa;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:18px;padding:36px 32px;
    display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:14px;position:relative;overflow:hidden;
    transition:background .4s var(--ease),transform .4s var(--ease);
  }
  .cs-cso .card:hover{background:#f4f4f4;transform:translateY(-3px)}
  .cs-cso .card .ix{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:34px;line-height:1;color:var(--ink-3);font-feature-settings:"tnum"}
  .cs-cso .card b{font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:500}
  .cs-cso .card h3{font-size:28px;font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.02em;line-height:1.1;color:var(--ink);text-wrap:balance}
  .cs-cso .card h3 em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink-2)}
  .cs-cso .card p{font-size:15px;line-height:1.55;color:var(--ink-2);text-wrap:pretty}
  .cs-cso .card .stat{margin-top:auto;padding-top:20px;border-top:1px dashed var(--line);display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:14px;flex-wrap:nowrap;white-space:nowrap}
  .cs-cso .card .stat b{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:32px;color:var(--ink);letter-spacing:-.02em;font-weight:400;line-height:1;flex-shrink:0}
  .cs-cso .card .stat span{font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);line-height:1.4;white-space:normal;flex:1;min-width:0}

  /* AI process — the 2026 signature section */
  .cs-ai{background:var(--ink);color:#fafafa;padding:140px 200px;position:relative;overflow:hidden}
  .cs-ai::before{content:"";position:absolute;top:-50%;left:-10%;width:60%;height:200%;background:radial-gradient(ellipse at center,rgba(198,251,80,.12) 0%,rgba(198,251,80,0) 60%);pointer-events:none}
  .cs-ai .cs-eyebrow{color:rgba(250,250,250,.6);margin-bottom:72px}
  .cs-ai .cs-eyebrow .num{background:var(--accent);color:var(--ink);box-shadow:0 0 0 5px rgba(198,251,80,.18)}
  .cs-ai .cs-eyebrow .line{background:rgba(250,250,250,.18)}
  .cs-ai h2{font-size:clamp(40px,4vw,64px);font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.025em;line-height:1.05;max-width:18ch;margin-bottom:18px;text-wrap:balance}
  .cs-ai h2 em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:var(--accent)}
  .cs-ai .ai-intro{font-size:17px;line-height:1.55;color:rgba(250,250,250,.65);max-width:640px;margin-bottom:64px;text-wrap:pretty}
  .cs-ai .ai-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:20px;position:relative;z-index:1}
  .cs-ai .ai-card{
    background:rgba(255,255,255,.04);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08);border-radius:14px;
    padding:24px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:14px;transition:border-color .35s var(--ease),background .35s var(--ease)
  }
  .cs-ai .ai-card:hover{border-color:rgba(198,251,80,.5);background:rgba(255,255,255,.06)}
  .cs-ai .ai-card .sparkle{width:24px;height:24px;color:var(--accent);filter:drop-shadow(0 0 8px rgba(198,251,80,.4));animation:aiTwinkle 3.4s ease-in-out infinite}
  .cs-ai .ai-card .sparkle svg{width:100%;height:100%;display:block}
  .cs-ai .ai-card h4{font-size:17px;font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.01em;line-height:1.25;color:#fff;text-wrap:balance}
  .cs-ai .ai-card p{font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.55;color:rgba(250,250,250,.6);text-wrap:pretty}

  /* Visual showcase */
  .cs-showcase{padding:0;border:0}
  .cs-showcase .spread{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:2px;background:var(--line)}
  .cs-showcase .spread img{width:100%;height:62vh;object-fit:cover;display:block}
  .cs-showcase .full{width:100%;height:90vh;object-fit:cover;display:block;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);border-top:1px solid var(--line)}

  /* AdvanceCM showcase — natural aspect for flat-lays (1 wide + 2 squares) */
  .case-study[data-case="advance-cm"] .cs-showcase .full{
    height:auto;aspect-ratio:2880/1573;object-fit:cover;background:#f4f4f0;
  }
  .case-study[data-case="advance-cm"] .cs-showcase .spread img{
    height:auto;aspect-ratio:1/1;object-fit:cover;background:#f4f4f0;
  }
  @media (max-width:760px){
    .case-study[data-case="advance-cm"] .cs-showcase .spread img{aspect-ratio:1/1;height:auto}
  }

  /* IREMOS showcase — dark mockup spread (1 wide + 2 squares) */
  .case-study[data-case="iremos"] .cs-showcase .full{
    height:auto;aspect-ratio:16/9;object-fit:cover;background:#0a0a0a;
  }
  .case-study[data-case="iremos"] .cs-showcase .spread img{
    height:auto;aspect-ratio:1/1;object-fit:cover;background:#0a0a0a;
  }
  @media (max-width:760px){
    .case-study[data-case="iremos"] .cs-showcase .spread img{aspect-ratio:1/1;height:auto}
  }

  /* Outcome stats */
  .cs-stats{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:0;border-top:1px solid var(--line)}
  .cs-stats .scell{padding:64px 48px;border-right:1px solid var(--line);position:relative}
  .cs-stats .scell:last-child{border-right:0}
  .cs-stats .scell b{display:block;font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);margin-bottom:24px;font-weight:500}
  .cs-stats .scell .num{font-size:clamp(54px,5vw,86px);font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.04em;line-height:.95;color:var(--ink);font-feature-settings:"tnum"}
  .cs-stats .scell .num em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;font-size:.5em;color:var(--accent);vertical-align:super;margin-left:4px}
  .cs-stats .scell p{margin-top:20px;font-size:14px;line-height:1.5;color:var(--ink-2);max-width:280px;text-wrap:pretty}

  /* Testimonial */
  .cs-testimonial{padding:140px 200px;background:#fafafa;text-align:center}
  .cs-testimonial blockquote{
    font-size:clamp(26px,2.4vw,40px);font-weight:400;letter-spacing:-.02em;line-height:1.3;
    max-width:980px;margin:0 auto;color:var(--ink);text-wrap:balance
  }
  .cs-testimonial blockquote em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink-2)}
  .cs-testimonial blockquote::before{content:"\201C";display:block;font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:120px;line-height:.8;color:var(--accent);margin-bottom:8px}
  .cs-testimonial .qmeta{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:16px;margin-top:40px}
  .cs-testimonial .qmeta .avatar{width:54px;height:54px;border-radius:50%;background-size:cover;background-position:center;border:2px solid #fff;box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.08)}
  .cs-testimonial .qmeta .who{text-align:left}
  .cs-testimonial .qmeta .who b{font-size:14px;font-weight:500;color:var(--ink);display:block}
  .cs-testimonial .qmeta .who span{font-size:12px;color:var(--ink-2)}

  /* Related work */
  .cs-related{padding:120px 200px 140px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);background:var(--bg)}
  .cs-related .cs-eyebrow{margin-bottom:48px}
  .cs-related .grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:32px}
  .cs-related .rcard{
    display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:18px;
    text-decoration:none;color:var(--ink);cursor:auto;
    transition:transform .5s var(--ease);
  }
  /* Safety net: never let the OS cursor leak inside the case-study overlay */
  .case-study,.case-study *{cursor:auto}
  @media (hover:none){.case-study,.case-study *{cursor:auto}}
  .cs-related .rcard:hover{transform:translateY(-6px)}
  .cs-related .rcard .thumb{
    aspect-ratio:1672/941;border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden;background:var(--bg-2);position:relative;
  }
  .cs-related .rcard .thumb img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;transition:transform 1.2s var(--ease)}
  .cs-related .rcard:hover .thumb img{transform:scale(1.05)}
  .cs-related .rcard .thumb::after{
    content:"";position:absolute;inset:0;background:linear-gradient(180deg,transparent 60%,rgba(0,0,0,.5));
    opacity:0;transition:opacity .4s var(--ease);
  }
  .cs-related .rcard:hover .thumb::after{opacity:1}
  .cs-related .rcard .thumb .open-cue{
    position:absolute;left:18px;bottom:18px;z-index:2;
    width:42px;height:42px;border-radius:50%;background:#fff;color:var(--ink);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px);transition:opacity .35s var(--ease),transform .45s var(--ease),background .35s,color .35s;
  }
  .cs-related .rcard:hover .open-cue{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0);background:var(--accent)}
  .cs-related .rcard .open-cue svg{width:16px;height:16px}
  .cs-related .rcard .pill{
    align-self:flex-start;font-size:10px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;
    padding:6px 12px;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:99px;color:var(--ink-2);font-weight:500;
  }
  .cs-related .rcard h3{font-size:26px;font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.02em;line-height:1.1;color:var(--ink);display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:12px;text-wrap:balance;margin:0}
  .cs-related .rcard h3 em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;color:var(--ink-2)}
  .cs-related .rcard h3 .ix{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-3);font-weight:400}
  .cs-related .rcard p{font-size:14px;line-height:1.55;color:var(--ink-2);max-width:38ch;text-wrap:pretty;margin:0}
  .cs-related .rcard .meta{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3);padding-top:14px;border-top:1px solid var(--line);margin-top:auto}
  .cs-related .rcard .meta em{font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;color:var(--ink-2);font-size:13px}
  @media (max-width:1280px){.cs-related{padding:100px 64px}.cs-related .grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:28px}.cs-related .rcard:nth-child(3){display:none}}
  @media (max-width:760px){.cs-related{padding:80px 28px}.cs-related .grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.cs-related .rcard:nth-child(3){display:flex}}

  /* Hide legacy single-next card */
  .cs-next{display:none !important}

  /* ─── Case Study — reveal animations & interactive states ─── */
  .cs-sec,.cs-ai,.cs-stats,.cs-testimonial,.cs-related,.cs-showcase{
    opacity:0;transform:translateY(28px);
    transition:opacity .9s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1),transform 1.05s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1);
  }
  .cs-sec.is-in,.cs-ai.is-in,.cs-stats.is-in,.cs-testimonial.is-in,.cs-related.is-in,.cs-showcase.is-in{opacity:1;transform:none}
  @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){
    .cs-sec,.cs-ai,.cs-stats,.cs-testimonial,.cs-related,.cs-showcase{opacity:1;transform:none;transition:none}
  }

  /* Project highlights — staggered card reveal + interactive hover */
  .cs-cso .card{opacity:0;transform:translateY(18px);transition:opacity .8s var(--ease),transform .55s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1),background .4s var(--ease),box-shadow .4s var(--ease);transition-delay:calc(var(--i,0) * 110ms)}
  .cs-sec.is-in .cs-cso .card{opacity:1;transform:none}
  .cs-cso .card:hover{background:#f4f4f4;transform:translateY(-6px);box-shadow:0 24px 50px -28px rgba(13,13,13,.18)}
  .cs-cso .card .ix{transition:color .4s var(--ease),transform .55s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)}
  .cs-cso .card:hover .ix{color:var(--accent-deep,#5fa314);transform:translateY(-2px) rotate(-2deg)}
  .cs-cso .card .stat b{display:inline-block;transition:color .4s var(--ease),transform .55s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)}
  .cs-cso .card:hover .stat b{color:var(--accent-deep,#5fa314);transform:scale(1.08)}

  /* AI cards — sequential reveal + per-card hover lift */
  .cs-ai .ai-card{opacity:0;transform:translateY(14px);transition:opacity .7s var(--ease),transform .55s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1),border-color .35s var(--ease),background .35s var(--ease),box-shadow .4s var(--ease);transition-delay:calc(var(--i,0) * 90ms + 200ms)}
  .cs-ai.is-in .ai-card{opacity:1;transform:none}
  .cs-ai .ai-card:hover{transform:translateY(-5px);border-color:rgba(198,251,80,.55);background:rgba(255,255,255,.07);box-shadow:0 20px 40px -24px rgba(198,251,80,.22)}
  .cs-ai .ai-card .sparkle{transition:transform .55s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1)}
  .cs-ai .ai-card:hover .sparkle{transform:rotate(12deg) scale(1.15)}

  /* Stats — staggered reveal + counter scale-up + hover */
  .cs-stats .scell{position:relative;overflow:hidden;opacity:0;transform:translateY(14px);transition:opacity .8s var(--ease),transform .8s var(--ease),background .4s var(--ease);transition-delay:calc(var(--i,0) * 110ms + 80ms)}
  .cs-stats.is-in .scell{opacity:1;transform:none}
  .cs-stats .scell::before{
    content:"";position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;height:2px;
    background:linear-gradient(90deg,var(--accent),transparent);
    transform:scaleX(0);transform-origin:left;transition:transform .8s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1);
  }
  .cs-stats.is-in .scell::before{transform:scaleX(1);transition-delay:calc(var(--i,0) * 120ms + 250ms)}
  .cs-stats .scell:hover{background:#fafafa}
  .cs-stats .scell .num{display:inline-block;transform:scale(.92);opacity:0;transition:opacity .7s var(--ease),transform .9s cubic-bezier(.34,1.56,.64,1),color .4s var(--ease)}
  .cs-stats.is-in .scell .num{opacity:1;transform:scale(1);transition-delay:calc(var(--i,0) * 120ms + 380ms)}
  .cs-stats .scell:hover .num{transform:scale(1.04)}
  .cs-stats .scell:hover .num em{color:var(--accent-deep,#5fa314)}

  /* Testimonial reveal */
  .cs-testimonial blockquote{opacity:0;transform:translateY(14px);transition:opacity .9s var(--ease) .15s,transform .9s var(--ease) .15s}
  .cs-testimonial.is-in blockquote{opacity:1;transform:none}
  .cs-testimonial .qmeta{opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px);transition:opacity .8s var(--ease) .45s,transform .8s var(--ease) .45s}
  .cs-testimonial.is-in .qmeta{opacity:1;transform:none}

  /* Related — card reveal stagger */
  .cs-related .rcard{opacity:0;transform:translateY(20px);transition:opacity .8s var(--ease),transform .55s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1);transition-delay:calc(var(--i,0) * 110ms + 140ms)}
  .cs-related.is-in .rcard{opacity:1;transform:none}
  .cs-related.is-in .rcard:hover{transform:translateY(-6px)}

  /* Intro paragraph reveal */
  .cs-intro p{opacity:0;transform:translateY(10px);transition:opacity .9s var(--ease) .15s,transform .9s var(--ease) .15s}
  .cs-sec.cs-intro.is-in p{opacity:1;transform:none}

  /* Meta row hover micro-interaction */
  .cs-meta .col{transition:transform .35s var(--ease)}
  .cs-meta .col:hover{transform:translateY(-2px)}
  .cs-meta .col b{transition:color .35s var(--ease)}
  .cs-meta .col:hover b{color:var(--accent-deep,#5fa314)}

  /* Showcase images — subtle zoom on hover */
  .cs-showcase img{transition:transform 1.2s cubic-bezier(.22,1,.36,1),filter .6s var(--ease)}
  .cs-showcase img:hover{transform:scale(1.02);filter:brightness(1.04)}

  /* Footer hint */
  .cs-foot{padding:32px clamp(28px,5vw,100px);border-top:1px solid var(--line);display:flex;justify-content:space-between;font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.22em;text-transform:uppercase;color:var(--ink-3)}
  .cs-foot kbd{
    display:inline-block;padding:3px 8px;background:#fafafa;border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px;
    font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-size:10px;font-weight:500;letter-spacing:.04em;color:var(--ink-2);margin:0 4px;
  }

  @media (max-width:1280px){
    .cs-sec,.cs-ai,.cs-testimonial{padding:100px 64px}
    .cs-meta{padding:40px 64px}
    .cs-next{padding:80px 64px}
    .cs-foot{padding:24px 64px}
    .cs-hero-text{left:32px;right:32px;bottom:40px}
    .cs-hero .cs-live{top:32px;right:32px}
    .cs-top{padding:16px 32px}
  }
  @media (max-width:900px){
    .cs-meta{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:24px}
    .cs-meta .cs-live{grid-column:1/-1;justify-self:start;margin-top:8px}
    .cs-cso{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .cs-ai .ai-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .cs-stats{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr}
    .cs-stats .scell:nth-child(2){border-right:0}
    .cs-stats .scell:nth-child(-n+2){border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
    .cs-showcase .spread{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .cs-sec,.cs-ai,.cs-testimonial{padding:80px 28px}
    .cs-meta{padding:32px 28px}
    .cs-top{grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;padding:14px 24px}
    .cs-index{display:none}
    .cs-title{justify-self:end;text-align:right}
  }


  /* ─── RESPONSIVE — Tablet & Mobile ─────────────────── */

  /* TABLET LANDSCAPE & SMALLER (≤1100px) */
  @media (max-width:1100px){
    /* Split — stack at 1100 instead of 960 */
    .split{grid-template-columns:1fr !important}
    .split-aside{display:none}
    .aside-stack{height:auto;min-height:200px}
    .rsec{padding:90px 40px !important}

    /* Hero */
    .hero{padding:200px 32px 100px}
    .hero-h1{font-size:clamp(48px,9vw,96px) !important}
    .hero-eyebrow{margin-bottom:80px}

    /* Works rows */
    .work{grid-template-columns:50px 1fr auto 70px 32px;gap:18px;padding:24px 0}
    .work .preview{display:none !important}
    .work:hover{padding:30px 20px}
    .work .title{font-size:36px}
    .work .meta{font-size:11px}

    /* Services */
    .srv{padding:28px 0}
    .srv .name{font-size:30px}

    /* Top thread nav — hide wire, compact labels */
    .thread-host{height:96px}
    .thread-svg{display:none}
    .thread-labels{display:flex;position:absolute;right:40px;top:30px;left:auto;bottom:auto;gap:22px;align-items:center}
    .thread-label{position:relative !important;left:auto !important;top:auto !important;transform:none !important;padding:6px 0;font-size:14px}
    .thread-label::after{display:none}
    .thread-label .lbl-num{display:none}
    .thread-label.is-active{transform:none !important}
    .thread-hand,.thread-hint{display:none !important}
    .thread-logo{top:28px;left:40px}
    .thread-logo img{height:40px}

    /* Footer */
    footer{padding:80px 40px 32px}
    .ft-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:48px}
    .ft-social{grid-column:1/-1}
    .ft-social ul{flex-direction:row;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:24px}
    .ft-mark{margin:0 -40px}

    /* Case study */
    .cs-sec,.cs-ai,.cs-testimonial{padding:80px 40px !important}
    .cs-meta{padding:32px 40px}
    .cs-related{padding:80px 40px}
    .cs-related .grid{grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:24px}
    .cs-related .rcard:nth-child(3){display:none}
    .cs-top{padding:16px 24px}
    .cs-hero-text{left:24px;right:24px;bottom:32px}
    .cs-hero .cs-live{top:24px;right:24px}

    /* Stats marquee — bit smaller */
    .footer-mq .track{font-size:72px}
  }

  /* TABLET PORTRAIT (≤768px) */
  @media (max-width:768px){
    /* Reduce thread-nav labels — only show essentials */
    .thread-labels{gap:14px;right:24px;top:26px}
    .thread-label{font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.04em}
    .thread-label[data-target="services"],
    .thread-label[data-target="process"],
    .thread-label[data-target="why"],
    .thread-label[data-target="words"]{display:none}
    .thread-logo{left:24px}
    .thread-logo img{height:34px}

    /* Hero scales down */
    .hero{padding:160px 24px 80px}
    .hero-eyebrow{margin-bottom:56px}
    .hero-eyebrow .ai-sparkle{width:12px;height:12px}
    .hero-h1{font-size:clamp(42px,11vw,80px) !important}
    .hero-grid{gap:36px}
    .hero-desc{font-size:16px}
    .hero-cta{padding:16px 24px;font-size:14px}
    .hero-cta-wrap{margin-top:20px;gap:16px}
    .hero-meta-row{font-size:11px}

    /* LogoLoop */
    .logoloop-item{font-size:22px}
    .logoloop-list{gap:36px;padding-right:36px}

    /* Split / aside */
    .split-aside{padding:48px 24px 32px}
    .aside-stack{min-height:160px}
    .aside-section h2{font-size:28px}
    .aside-section .num{font-size:96px}
    .aside-section .desc{max-width:none}
    .rsec{padding:64px 24px !important}

    /* Works rows — collapse meta */
    .work{grid-template-columns:40px 1fr 28px;gap:14px;padding:18px 0}
    .work .meta,.work .year{display:none}
    .work .title{font-size:24px;letter-spacing:-.02em}
    .work .title em{font-size:.45em}
    .work:hover{padding:22px 14px}
    .work .idx{width:36px;height:36px;font-size:18px}
    .work .go{width:32px;height:32px}

    /* Services */
    .srv{grid-template-columns:40px 1fr 36px;gap:16px;padding:22px 0}
    .srv .desc{display:none}
    .srv .name{font-size:22px}

    /* Process/Benefits grids stack */
    .process-grid,.benefits-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .pcell{padding:32px 24px;min-height:auto}
    .pcell .pnum{font-size:44px}
    .pcell h3{font-size:24px}
    .benefit{padding:28px 22px}

    /* Testimonials */
    .quote{padding:40px 0}
    .quote blockquote{font-size:22px}
    .quote .qmark{font-size:80px;top:24px}

    /* Contact */
    .contact-inner{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:36px}
    .contact h2{font-size:48px}
    .form{padding:28px 24px}

    /* Chat FAQ inside contact */
    .faq-block{margin-top:64px;padding-top:48px}
    .faq-block-h{font-size:32px;margin-bottom:36px}
    .msg{max-width:94%}
    .q-bubble,.ans-bubble{font-size:14px;padding:14px 18px}
    .chat-feed{gap:14px}
    .ans-row{max-width:94%}
    .ans-row > .ava{width:46px;height:46px;min-width:46px;min-height:46px;margin-right:12px}
    .chat::before,.chat::after{display:none}
    .chat-compose{padding:18px 20px;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:14px}
    .chat-compose .cc-send{width:100%;justify-content:space-between}

    /* Footer */
    footer{padding:64px 24px 28px}
    .ft-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:36px;margin-bottom:36px}
    .ft-studio h3{font-size:32px}
    .ft-time b{font-size:24px}
    .ft-mark{margin:0 -24px}
    .ft-bot{flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:12px;text-align:left}
    .ft-social ul,.ft-nav ul{flex-direction:column;gap:10px}

    /* Marquee */
    .footer-mq .track{font-size:52px;padding:32px 0 20px}
    .footer-mq .track .phrase em{font-size:.18em}

    /* Section eyebrows */
    .eyebrow{margin-bottom:32px;flex-wrap:wrap}
    .eyebrow .end{display:none}

    /* Case study mobile */
    .cs-top{grid-template-columns:auto 1fr;padding:14px 20px}
    .cs-title em,.cs-index{display:none}
    .cs-title{justify-self:end;text-align:right}
    .cs-back{font-size:11px}
    .cs-back .arr{width:28px;height:28px}
    .cs-hero{height:70vh;min-height:440px}
    .cs-hero-text{left:20px;right:20px;bottom:24px;gap:14px}
    .cs-hero-text h2{font-size:clamp(36px,9vw,64px)}
    .cs-hero-text .kicker{font-size:16px;line-height:1.35}
    .cs-hero .cs-live{top:20px;right:20px;padding:8px 14px;font-size:10px}
    .cs-meta{column-gap:32px;row-gap:18px;padding:28px 20px}
    .cs-meta .col{max-width:none}
    .cs-meta .cs-live{margin-left:0;width:100%;justify-content:center;margin-top:4px}
    .cs-meta .col span{font-size:14px}
    .cs-sec,.cs-ai,.cs-testimonial{padding:60px 20px !important}
    .cs-intro p{font-size:18px}
    .cs-cso{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:16px}
    .cs-cso .card{padding:24px 22px}
    .cs-cso .card h3{font-size:22px}
    .cs-cso .card .stat b{font-size:32px}
    .cs-ai h2{font-size:32px}
    .cs-ai .ai-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .cs-stats{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .cs-stats .scell{padding:36px 28px;border-right:0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line)}
    .cs-stats .scell:last-child{border-bottom:0}
    .cs-stats .scell .num{font-size:64px}
    .cs-showcase .full{height:50vh}
    .cs-showcase .spread{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .cs-showcase .spread img{height:40vh}
    .cs-testimonial blockquote{font-size:22px}
    .cs-testimonial blockquote::before{font-size:72px}
    .cs-testimonial .qmeta{flex-direction:column;text-align:center}
    .cs-related{padding:64px 20px}
    .cs-related .grid{grid-template-columns:1fr;gap:24px}
    .cs-related .rcard:nth-child(3){display:flex}
  }

  /* MOBILE (≤480px) */
  @media (max-width:480px){
    .hero{padding:130px 18px 64px}
    .hero-h1{font-size:clamp(36px,12vw,64px) !important}
    .hero-eyebrow{font-size:9.5px;letter-spacing:.18em;padding:8px 14px 8px 10px}
    .hero-eyebrow{margin-bottom:48px}
    .hero-desc{font-size:15px}

    .thread-logo img{height:30px}
    .thread-labels{gap:10px;right:18px}
    .thread-label{font-size:11px}

    .work .title{font-size:20px}
    .srv .name{font-size:18px}

    .pcell{padding:24px 18px}
    .benefit{padding:22px 18px}
    .form{padding:22px 18px}

    .contact h2{font-size:38px}
    .faq-block-h{font-size:26px}

    .footer-mq .track{font-size:38px}
    .footer-mq .track .phrase em{font-size:.2em}

    footer{padding:48px 18px 24px}
    .ft-studio h3{font-size:26px}
    .ft-mark{margin:0 -18px 0 0}
    .ft-mark img{width:min(86%,440px)}

    .logoloop-item{font-size:18px}
    .logoloop-list{gap:24px}

    /* Disable cursor follow on touch — already handled but reaffirm */
    body{cursor:auto !important}
  }

  /* Touch devices — disable hover-dependent transforms that can stick */
  @media (hover:none){
    body{cursor:auto}
    .work:hover{padding:28px 16px}
    .work:hover .preview{display:none}
    .srv:hover{background:transparent}
    .ft-nav a:hover,.ft-social a:hover{transform:none}
  }


  /* ─── Mobile menu (creative, full-screen drawer) ─── */
  .mm-toggle{display:none}
  .mm-overlay{display:none}

  @media (max-width:1100px){
    /* Hide simplified label list, show hamburger instead */
    .thread-labels{display:none !important}

    .mm-toggle{
      display:flex;position:fixed;top:28px;right:40px;z-index:9700;
      align-items:center;gap:10px;
      background:rgba(255,255,255,.92);backdrop-filter:saturate(1.4) blur(10px);
      border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:99px;
      padding:10px 16px 10px 14px;
      font-family:inherit;font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;
      color:var(--ink);cursor:pointer;font-weight:500;
      transition:background .3s var(--ease),color .3s var(--ease),border-color .3s var(--ease);
    }
    .mm-toggle:hover,.mm-toggle.is-open{background:var(--ink);color:#fff;border-color:var(--ink)}
    .mm-bars{display:inline-flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;width:14px}
    .mm-bars span{display:block;height:1.5px;background:currentColor;border-radius:2px;transition:transform .35s var(--ease),width .35s var(--ease)}
    .mm-bars span:first-child{width:14px}
    .mm-bars span:last-child{width:10px;align-self:flex-end}
    .mm-toggle:hover .mm-bars span:last-child,.mm-toggle.is-open .mm-bars span:last-child{width:14px}
    .mm-toggle.is-open .mm-bars span:first-child{transform:translateY(3px) rotate(45deg)}
    .mm-toggle.is-open .mm-bars span:last-child{transform:translateY(-3px) rotate(-45deg)}

    .mm-overlay{
      display:block;position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:9600;
      background:var(--ink);color:#fafafa;
      transform:translateY(-100%);transition:transform .75s cubic-bezier(.7,.05,.2,1);
      overflow:hidden;
      pointer-events:none;
    }
    .mm-overlay.is-open{transform:translateY(0);pointer-events:auto}

    .mm-bg{position:absolute;inset:0;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-end;padding-right:-10%;overflow:hidden;pointer-events:none}
    .mm-mark{
      width:75vw;max-width:780px;height:auto;
      filter:invert(1) brightness(1.05);
      opacity:.06;
      transform:translateX(28%) rotate(-12deg);
      transition:transform 1.2s var(--ease),opacity 1.2s var(--ease);
    }
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-mark{transform:translateX(18%) rotate(-8deg);opacity:.08}

    .mm-nav{
      position:relative;z-index:2;
      display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;
      padding:140px 40px 0;
    }
    .mm-item{
      display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:24px;
      text-decoration:none;color:#fafafa;cursor:pointer;
      padding:14px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08);
      opacity:0;transform:translateY(20px);
      transition:opacity .65s var(--ease),transform .7s var(--ease),color .3s,padding-left .4s var(--ease);
    }
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-item{opacity:1;transform:none}
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-item:nth-child(1){transition-delay:.18s}
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-item:nth-child(2){transition-delay:.24s}
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-item:nth-child(3){transition-delay:.30s}
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-item:nth-child(4){transition-delay:.36s}
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-item:nth-child(5){transition-delay:.42s}
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-item:nth-child(6){transition-delay:.48s}
    .mm-item:hover,.mm-item.is-pressed{color:var(--accent);padding-left:8px}
    .mm-item .mm-num{
      font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;
      font-size:18px;color:rgba(250,250,250,.35);min-width:32px;
      transition:color .3s;
    }
    .mm-item:hover .mm-num{color:var(--accent)}
    .mm-item .mm-name{
      font-family:"Onest",sans-serif;font-weight:500;
      font-size:clamp(40px,9vw,72px);letter-spacing:-.025em;line-height:1;
    }
    .mm-item:nth-child(2) .mm-name,.mm-item:nth-child(4) .mm-name,.mm-item:nth-child(6) .mm-name{
      font-family:"Instrument Serif",serif;font-style:italic;font-weight:400;letter-spacing:-.01em;
    }

    .mm-foot{
      position:absolute;left:40px;right:40px;bottom:40px;z-index:2;
      display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:16px;
      padding-top:24px;border-top:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08);
      font-size:11px;letter-spacing:.18em;text-transform:uppercase;color:rgba(250,250,250,.55);
      opacity:0;transition:opacity .6s var(--ease) .55s;
    }
    .mm-overlay.is-open .mm-foot{opacity:1}
    .mm-status{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
    .mm-status .dot{width:7px;height:7px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--accent);box-shadow:0 0 0 0 rgba(198,251,80,.5);animation:livepulse 2.4s ease-out infinite}
    .mm-email{color:#fafafa;text-decoration:none;text-transform:none;letter-spacing:0;font-size:14px;font-weight:400}
  }

  @media (max-width:640px){
    .mm-toggle{top:22px;right:20px}
    .mm-nav{padding:120px 24px 0;gap:4px}
    .mm-item{padding:12px 0}
    .mm-item .mm-name{font-size:clamp(34px,11vw,56px)}
    .mm-foot{left:24px;right:24px;bottom:24px;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px}
  }


  /* ════════════ Selected Work — stacked scroll showcase ════════════ */
  .rsec--works{padding-right:clamp(40px,5vw,80px) !important}
  .works-stack{position:relative;display:block;padding-top:10px;max-width:1240px;margin-inline:auto}
  .ws-card{
    position:sticky;top:calc(86px + var(--idx,0) * 18px);
    display:flex;flex-direction:column;
    background:#0d0d0d;color:#fafafa;cursor:auto;
    border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.07);border-radius:24px;overflow:hidden;
    margin-bottom:clamp(26px,3.4vw,52px);
    box-shadow:0 40px 90px -60px rgba(13,13,13,.7);
    transform-origin:50% 0;will-change:transform;
  }
  .ws-card:last-child{margin-bottom:0}

  /* content reveal base (panel only — thumbnail stays visible, no fade) */
  .ws-card .ws-panel > *{opacity:0;transform:translateY(16px);
    transition:opacity .7s var(--ease),transform .7s var(--ease)}
  .ws-card.is-in .ws-panel > *{opacity:1;transform:none}
  .ws-card.is-in .ws-panel > *:nth-child(1){transition-delay:.12s}
  .ws-card.is-in .ws-panel > *:nth-child(2){transition-delay:.20s}
  .ws-card.is-in .ws-panel > *:nth-child(3){transition-delay:.28s}

  /* header */
  .ws-head{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr auto;align-items:center;gap:24px;
    padding:22px clamp(22px,2.6vw,38px);border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.08);position:relative;z-index:2}
  .ws-eyebrow{display:flex;align-items:baseline;gap:16px;min-width:0}
  .ws-idx{font-size:12px;letter-spacing:.12em;color:rgba(255,255,255,.42);font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;white-space:nowrap}
  .ws-idx b{color:var(--accent);font-weight:600}
  .ws-name{font-size:clamp(20px,2vw,27px);font-weight:500;letter-spacing:-.022em;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis}
  .ws-view{justify-self:end;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;white-space:nowrap;
    font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:#0d0d0d;background:var(--accent);border-radius:999px;padding:11px 18px;
    transition:transform .4s var(--ease)}
  .ws-view svg{width:15px;height:15px;transition:transform .4s var(--ease)}
  .ws-card:hover .ws-view{transform:translateY(-1px)}
  .ws-card:hover .ws-view svg{transform:translateX(3px)}

  /* stage / media */
  .ws-stage{position:relative;aspect-ratio:1672/941;overflow:hidden;
    background:radial-gradient(120% 130% at 50% 0,#1d1d1c 0,#0d0d0d 68%)}
