  // ─── Renderers ─────────────────────────────────
  const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);

  function setText(id, html){ const el = $(id); if(el) el.innerHTML = html; }
  function setAttr(id, attr, val){ const el = $(id); if(el) el.setAttribute(attr, val); }

  function renderCase(slug){
    const c = CASES[slug];
    if (!c) return;
    setText('csTitleName', c.name);
    setText('csTitleYear', ' · ' + c.year);
    setText('csTitleIdx', c.idx);
    setAttr('csHeroImg','src', img(slug, 0, 2000));
    setAttr('csHeroImg','alt', c.name);
    setText('csIndustryPill', c.industry);
    setText('csName', c.name);
    setText('csKicker', c.kicker);
    (function(){ const wrap=$('csActions'); if(!wrap) return;
      const APPLE='<svg viewBox="0 0 384 512" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M318.7 268.7c-.2-36.7 16.4-64.4 50-84.8-18.8-26.9-47.2-41.7-84.7-44.6-35.5-2.8-74.3 20.7-88.5 20.7-15 0-49.4-19.7-76.4-19.7C63.3 141.2 4 184.8 4 273.5q0 39.3 14.4 81.2c12.8 36.7 59 126.7 107.2 125.2 25.2-.6 43-17.9 75.8-17.9 31.8 0 48.3 17.9 76.4 17.9 48.6-.7 90.4-82.5 102.6-119.3-65.2-30.7-61.7-90-61.7-91.9zm-56.6-164.2c27.3-32.4 24.8-61.9 24-72.5-24.1 1.4-52 16.4-67.9 34.9-17.5 19.8-27.8 44.3-25.6 71.9 26.1 2 49.9-11.4 69.5-34.3z"/></svg>';
      const PLAY='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M3.6 2.3c-.3.2-.5.6-.5 1.1v17.2c0 .5.2.9.5 1.1l.1.1L13.4 12 3.7 2.2l-.1.1z"/><path d="M17.1 15.3 13.4 12l3.7-3.3 4.4 2.5c.9.5.9 1.6 0 2.1l-4.4 2z" opacity=".85"/><path d="M3.7 2.2 17.1 8.7 13.4 12 3.7 2.2z" opacity=".55"/><path d="M3.7 21.8 13.4 12l3.7 3.3-13.4 6.5z" opacity=".7"/></svg>';
      const store=(href,small,big,icon)=>'<a class="cs-store" href="'+href+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-cursor="link">'+icon+'<span class="lbl"><small>'+small+'</small><b>'+big+'</b></span></a>';
      const site=(href)=>'<a class="cs-live" href="'+href+'" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-cursor="link"><span>View site</span><span class="arrow" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round"><path d="M7 17 17 7M9 7h8v8"/></svg></span></a>';
      let html='';
      if (c.apps && (c.apps.ios || c.apps.android)) {
        if (c.apps.ios) html += store(c.apps.ios,'Download on the','App Store',APPLE);
        if (c.apps.android) html += store(c.apps.android,'Get it on','Google Play',PLAY);
      } else if (c.live && c.live !== '#') {
        html = site(c.live);
      }
      wrap.innerHTML = html;
      wrap.style.display = html ? '' : 'none';
    })();
    setText('csIndustry', c.industry);
    setText('csServices', c.services);
    setText('csYear', c.year);
    setText('csTimeline', c.timeline);
    setText('csRole', c.role);
    setText('csIntro', c.intro);
    setText('csChalH', c.challenge.h); setText('csChalP', c.challenge.p); setText('csChalStat', c.challenge.stat); setText('csChalStatLabel', c.challenge.label);
    setText('csSolH',  c.solution.h);  setText('csSolP',  c.solution.p);  setText('csSolStat',  c.solution.stat);  setText('csSolStatLabel',  c.solution.label);
    setText('csOutH',  c.outcome.h);   setText('csOutP',  c.outcome.p);   setText('csOutStat',  c.outcome.stat);   setText('csOutStatLabel',  c.outcome.label);

    setText('csAiHead', c.ai.head);
    setText('csAiIntro', c.ai.intro);
    const sparkleSvg = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="currentColor"><path d="M12 2 13.6 7c.4 1.2 1.2 2 2.4 2.4L21 11c.4.2.4.8 0 1L16 13.6c-1.2.4-2 1.2-2.4 2.4L12 21c-.2.4-.8.4-1 0L9.4 16c-.4-1.2-1.2-2-2.4-2.4L2 12c-.4-.2-.4-.8 0-1L7 9.4c1.2-.4 2-1.2 2.4-2.4L11 2c.2-.4.8-.4 1 0Z"/></svg>';
    $('csAiGrid').innerHTML = c.ai.cards.map(card => `
      <div class="ai-card">
        <span class="sparkle">${sparkleSvg}</span>
        <h4>${card.h}</h4>
        <p>${card.p}</p>
      </div>`).join('');

    $('csStats').innerHTML = c.stats.map(s => `
      <div class="scell">
        <b>${s.label}</b>
        <div class="num">${s.num}</div>
        <p>${s.p}</p>
      </div>`).join('');

    setText('csQuote', '\u201C' + c.quote.q + '\u201D');
    setText('csQAuthor', c.quote.author);
    setText('csQRole', c.quote.role);
    (function(){ const av=$('csQAvatar'); if(!av) return;
      if (c.quote.avatar){ av.style.display=''; av.style.backgroundImage = `url('${c.quote.avatar}')`; }
      else { av.style.display='none'; av.style.backgroundImage='none'; }
    })();

    setAttr('csShot1','src', img(slug, 1, 1800));
    setAttr('csShot2','src', img(slug, 2, 1200));
    setAttr('csShot3','src', img(slug, 3, 1200));

    const slugs = Object.keys(CASES).filter(s => !s.startsWith('_'));
    const here = slugs.indexOf(slug);
    const arrow = '<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"><path d="M7 17 17 7M9 7h8v8"/></svg>';
    const related = [1,2,3].map(i => slugs[(here + i) % slugs.length]);
    document.getElementById('csRelated').innerHTML = related.map(s => {
      const r = CASES[s];
      const shortAbout = r.kicker.length > 110 ? r.kicker.slice(0,108).trimEnd() + '…' : r.kicker;
      return `<a class="rcard" data-case-link="${s}">
        <div class="thumb">
          <img decoding="async" src="${workThumb(s)}" width="1672" height="941" alt="${r.name}" loading="lazy" />
          <span class="open-cue">${arrow}</span>
        </div>
        <span class="pill">${r.industry}</span>
        <h3>${r.name}</h3>
        <p>${shortAbout}</p>
        <div class="meta"><span>${r.services}</span><em>${r.year}</em></div>
      </a>`;
    }).join('');
    document.querySelectorAll('#csRelated [data-case-link]').forEach(el => {
      el.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.preventDefault(); openCase(el.dataset.caseLink); });
    });

    overlay.scrollTop = 0;
    $('csProgress').style.height = '0';
  }

  // ─── Reveal animations inside the overlay ─────────────────────
  // Sections (cs-sec, cs-ai, cs-stats, cs-testimonial, cs-related, cs-showcase) get .is-in via IO.
  // Children (.cs-cso .card, .cs-ai .ai-card, .cs-stats .scell, .cs-related .rcard) get --i for stagger.
  let csIO = null;
  function setupReveal(){
    // Assign indices once
    overlay.querySelectorAll('.cs-cso .card').forEach((el,i) => el.style.setProperty('--i', i));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('.cs-ai .ai-card').forEach((el,i) => el.style.setProperty('--i', i));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('.cs-stats .scell').forEach((el,i) => el.style.setProperty('--i', i));
    overlay.querySelectorAll('.cs-related .rcard').forEach((el,i) => el.style.setProperty('--i', i));

    if (csIO) csIO.disconnect();
    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    const targets = overlay.querySelectorAll('.cs-sec,.cs-ai,.cs-stats,.cs-testimonial,.cs-related,.cs-showcase');
    if (reduce){ targets.forEach(t => t.classList.add('is-in')); return; }
    targets.forEach(t => t.classList.remove('is-in'));
    csIO = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      entries.forEach(e => {
        if (e.isIntersecting){
          e.target.classList.add('is-in');
          csIO.unobserve(e.target);
        }
      });
    }, { root: overlay, threshold: 0.12, rootMargin: '0px 0px -6% 0px' });
    targets.forEach(t => csIO.observe(t));
    // Force-show first two sections after a tick so meta+hero feel immediate
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      const intro = overlay.querySelector('.cs-intro');
      if (intro) intro.classList.add('is-in');
    });
  }

  let csReturnFocus = null;
  function openCase(slug){
    csReturnFocus = document.activeElement;
    renderCase(slug);
    overlay.dataset.case = slug;
    document.body.classList.add('cs-locked');
    overlay.classList.add('is-open');
    overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
    overlay.scrollTop = 0;
    setupReveal();
    // Pause Lenis on background while overlay is open
    if (window.lenis && window.lenis.stop) window.lenis.stop();
    // Move focus into the dialog
    const back = document.getElementById('csBack');
    if (back) requestAnimationFrame(() => back.focus());
  }
  function closeCase(){
    overlay.classList.remove('is-open');
    overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    document.body.classList.remove('cs-locked');
    if (csIO){ csIO.disconnect(); csIO = null; }
    if (window.lenis && window.lenis.start) window.lenis.start();
    // Restore focus to the element that opened the dialog
    if (csReturnFocus && csReturnFocus.focus) { try { csReturnFocus.focus(); } catch(e){} }
    csReturnFocus = null;
  }
  // Trap Tab within the case-study dialog while open
  overlay.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Tab' || !overlay.classList.contains('is-open')) return;
    const f = overlay.querySelectorAll('a[href],button:not([disabled]),input,textarea,select,[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])');
    const vis = Array.from(f).filter(el => el.offsetParent !== null);
    if (!vis.length) return;
    const first = vis[0], last = vis[vis.length - 1];
    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first){ e.preventDefault(); last.focus(); }
    else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last){ e.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
  });

  // Wire .work clicks
  document.querySelectorAll('.work[data-case]').forEach(el => {
    el.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault();
      openCase(el.dataset.case);
    });
  });

  // ─── Benefits — staggered scroll-in ─────────────────
  (function benefitsReveal(){
    const grid = document.getElementById('benGrid');
    if (!grid) return;
    const cards = [...grid.querySelectorAll('.ben-card')];
    cards.forEach((c, i) => c.style.setProperty('--i', i));
    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    if (reduce){ cards.forEach(c => c.classList.add('is-in')); return; }
    const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries) => {
      entries.forEach(e => {
        if (e.isIntersecting){
          e.target.classList.add('is-in');
          io.unobserve(e.target);
        }
      });
    }, { threshold: 0.18, rootMargin: '0px 0px -8% 0px' });
    cards.forEach(c => io.observe(c));
  })();

  // ─── Testimonials v2 — Approval Ledger ────────────────────────
  (function wsecApprovalLedger(){
    const sec = document.getElementById('words');
    if (!sec) return;
    const root = document.getElementById('wsecRoot');
    const rows = [...sec.querySelectorAll('.wsec-row')];
    const quotes = [...sec.querySelectorAll('.wsec-q')];
    const auths = [...sec.querySelectorAll('.wsec-auth')];
    if (!rows.length) return;

    // Set per-row stagger index for the entrance animation
    rows.forEach((r, i) => r.style.setProperty('--i', i));

    let current = 0;
    let rotateTimer = 0;
    let userInteracted = false;

    function setActive(idx){
      if (idx === current) return;
      current = idx;
      rows.forEach((r, i) => {
        const on = i === idx;
        r.classList.toggle('is-active', on);
        r.setAttribute('aria-pressed', on ? 'true' : 'false');
      });
      quotes.forEach((q, i) => q.classList.toggle('is-active', i === idx));
      auths.forEach((a, i) => a.classList.toggle('is-active', i === idx));
    }

    function stopRotation(){
      if (rotateTimer){ clearInterval(rotateTimer); rotateTimer = 0; }
      userInteracted = true;
    }

    rows.forEach((row, i) => {
      row.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { stopRotation(); setActive(i); });
      row.addEventListener('click',      () => { stopRotation(); setActive(i); });
      row.addEventListener('focus',      () => { stopRotation(); setActive(i); });
      row.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' '){
          e.preventDefault(); stopRotation(); setActive(i);
        } else if (e.key === 'ArrowDown' || e.key === 'ArrowRight'){
          e.preventDefault();
          rows[Math.min(rows.length - 1, i + 1)].focus();
        } else if (e.key === 'ArrowUp' || e.key === 'ArrowLeft'){
          e.preventDefault();
          rows[Math.max(0, i - 1)].focus();
        }
      });
    });

    // Section-enter reveal + gentle auto-rotation until the user interacts
    if ('IntersectionObserver' in window){
      const io = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
        entries.forEach(e => {
          if (e.isIntersecting){
            sec.classList.add('is-revealed');
            // After the entrance settles, start a slow rotation
            if (!userInteracted && !rotateTimer){
              setTimeout(() => {
                if (userInteracted) return;
                rotateTimer = setInterval(() => {
                  if (userInteracted) return;
                  setActive((current + 1) % rows.length);
                }, 5200);
              }, 2000);
            }
            io.disconnect();
          }
        });
      }, { threshold: 0.18 });
      io.observe(sec);
    } else {
      sec.classList.add('is-revealed');
    }
  })();

  // ─── Process timeline — scroll-driven progress ────────────────
  (function procTimeline(){
    const root = document.getElementById('procTimeline');
    if (!root) return;
    const list = root.querySelector('.proc-list');
    const rows = [...root.querySelectorAll('.proc-row')];
    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

    if (reduce){
      root.style.setProperty('--fill', '1');
      root.style.setProperty('--pin-scale', '0');
      rows.forEach(r => r.classList.add('is-passed'));
      root.classList.add('is-complete');
      return;
    }

    let raf = 0;
    function update(){
      raf = 0;
      if (!list) return;
      const vh = window.innerHeight;
      // Anchor near 62% down the viewport
      const trigger = vh * 0.62;
      // Compute fill against the list's vertical span (rail is inside the list)
      const listRect = list.getBoundingClientRect();
      const total = listRect.height;
      if (total <= 0) return;
      const passed = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (trigger - listRect.top) / total));
      root.style.setProperty('--fill', passed.toFixed(4));
      root.classList.toggle('has-fill', passed > 0.02 && passed < 0.995);
      root.style.setProperty('--pin-scale', passed > 0.02 && passed < 0.995 ? '1' : '0');

      // Activate rows whose node center is at/above the trigger line
      let lastPassedIdx = -1;
      rows.forEach((row, i) => {
        const node = row.querySelector('.proc-node');
        if (!node) return;
        const nr = node.getBoundingClientRect();
        const nc = nr.top + nr.height / 2;
        const isPassed = nc <= trigger;
        row.classList.toggle('is-passed', isPassed);
        if (isPassed) lastPassedIdx = i;
      });

      // Tag the most-recently-passed row as the "current" phase
      rows.forEach((row, i) => {
        row.classList.toggle('is-current', i === lastPassedIdx && lastPassedIdx < rows.length - 1);
      });

      // End sign-off appears once all nodes passed
      const isComplete = lastPassedIdx >= rows.length - 1;
      root.classList.toggle('is-complete', isComplete);
    }
    function onScroll(){
      if (raf) return;
      raf = requestAnimationFrame(update);
    }
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll, { passive: true });
    window.addEventListener('resize', onScroll, { passive: true });
    update();
  })();

  // ─── Services accordion ────────────────────────────────────────
  (function srvAcc(){
    const root = document.getElementById('srvAcc');
    if (!root) return;
    const rows = [...root.querySelectorAll('.srv-row')];
    function toggle(row, force){
      const head = row.querySelector('.srv-head');
      const isOpen = row.classList.contains('is-open');
      const willOpen = force !== undefined ? force : !isOpen;
      if (willOpen){
        // Close others (accordion behavior)
        rows.forEach(r => {
          if (r !== row && r.classList.contains('is-open')){
            r.classList.remove('is-open');
            r.querySelector('.srv-head').setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
          }
        });
        row.classList.add('is-open');
        head.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
      } else {
        row.classList.remove('is-open');
        head.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
      }
    }
    rows.forEach(row => {
      const head = row.querySelector('.srv-head');
      head.addEventListener('click', () => toggle(row));
    });
  })();

  // ─── Selected Work — stacked scroll showcase ─────────────────────
  (function worksStack(){
    const stack = document.getElementById('worksStack');
    if (!stack) return;
    const cards = [...stack.querySelectorAll('.ws-card')];
    const reduce = window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;

    // Staggered content reveal as each card enters
    if (reduce){
      cards.forEach(c => c.classList.add('is-in'));
    } else {
      const io = new IntersectionObserver((entries)=>{
        entries.forEach(e=>{ if (e.isIntersecting){ e.target.classList.add('is-in'); io.unobserve(e.target); } });
      }, { threshold: 0.22, rootMargin: '0px 0px -8% 0px' });
      cards.forEach(c => io.observe(c));

      // Stacking depth — each card eases down in scale + brightness as the next covers it
      if (window.gsap && window.ScrollTrigger){
        cards.forEach((card, i)=>{
          if (i === cards.length - 1) return;
          gsap.to(card, {
            scale: 0.985, ease: 'none',
            scrollTrigger: { trigger: cards[i+1], start: 'top bottom', end: 'top top', scrub: true }
          });
        });
        ScrollTrigger.refresh();
      }
    }

    // Whole card opens the case study (keyboard + pointer)
    cards.forEach(card=>{
      const slug = card.dataset.case;
      const go = ()=>{ if (typeof openCase === 'function') openCase(slug); };
      card.addEventListener('click', (e)=>{ if (e.target.closest('a')) return; go(); });
      card.addEventListener('keydown', (e)=>{ if (e.key === 'Enter' || e.key === ' '){ e.preventDefault(); go(); } });
    });
  })();

  // Close handlers
  $('csBack').addEventListener('click', closeCase);
  document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && overlay.classList.contains('is-open')) closeCase();
  });

  // Capture wheel/touch BEFORE Lenis can preventDefault — let native overlay scroll work
  ['wheel','touchstart','touchmove'].forEach(evt => {
    overlay.addEventListener(evt, (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); }, { capture: true, passive: true });
  });

  // Scroll progress
  overlay.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
    const total = overlay.scrollHeight - overlay.clientHeight;
    const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, overlay.scrollTop / Math.max(1, total)));
    $('csProgress').style.height = (pct * 100) + '%';
  }, { passive: true });
})();
</script>



<script>
/* Mobile menu — creative drawer */
(function(){
  const toggle = document.getElementById('mmToggle');
  const overlay = document.getElementById('mmOverlay');
  if (!toggle || !overlay) return;
  const items = overlay.querySelectorAll('.mm-item[data-jump]');
  let mmReturnFocus = null;

  function open(){
    mmReturnFocus = document.activeElement;
    overlay.classList.add('is-open');
    overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
    toggle.classList.add('is-open');
    toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','true');
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    if (window.lenis && window.lenis.stop) window.lenis.stop();
    if (items.length) requestAnimationFrame(() => items[0].focus());
  }
  function close(){
    overlay.classList.remove('is-open');
    overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    toggle.classList.remove('is-open');
    toggle.setAttribute('aria-expanded','false');
    document.body.style.overflow = '';
    if (window.lenis && window.lenis.start) window.lenis.start();
    if (mmReturnFocus && mmReturnFocus.focus){ try { mmReturnFocus.focus(); } catch(e){} }
    mmReturnFocus = null;
  }
  overlay.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Tab' || !overlay.classList.contains('is-open')) return;
    const f = overlay.querySelectorAll('a[href],button:not([disabled]),[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])');
    const vis = Array.from(f).filter(el => el.offsetParent !== null);
    if (!vis.length) return;
    const first = vis[0], last = vis[vis.length - 1];
    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first){ e.preventDefault(); last.focus(); }
    else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last){ e.preventDefault(); first.focus(); }
  });
  toggle.addEventListener('click', () => {
    overlay.classList.contains('is-open') ? close() : open();
  });
  document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape' && overlay.classList.contains('is-open')) close();
  });
  items.forEach(el => {
    el.addEventListener('click', e => {
      e.preventDefault();
      el.classList.add('is-pressed');
      const target = document.getElementById(el.dataset.jump);
      setTimeout(() => {
        close();
        if (target) {
          const vh = window.innerHeight || 1;
          const top = target.getBoundingClientRect().top + window.scrollY - vh * 0.18;
          window.scrollTo({ top: Math.max(0, top), behavior: 'smooth' });
        }
        setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('is-pressed'), 600);
      }, 220);
    });
  });
  // Prevent body wheel scroll from leaking into Lenis while open
  ['wheel','touchstart','touchmove'].forEach(evt => {
    overlay.addEventListener(evt, e => e.stopPropagation(), { capture: true, passive: true });
  });
})();
</script>

<!-- ─── Premium custom cursor (isolated so nothing else can stop it) ─── -->
<script>
(function premiumCursor(){
  try{
    if (window.matchMedia && matchMedia('(any-pointer:fine)').matches === false) return; // no precise pointer -> native cursor
    const cur = document.getElementById('cur');
    const label = document.getElementById('curLabel');
    if (!cur) return;
    document.documentElement.classList.add('cur-on');
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', e => {                 // 1:1 follow — no lag
      cur.style.transform = 'translate3d(' + e.clientX + 'px,' + e.clientY + 'px,0)';
      cur.style.opacity = '1';
    }, {passive:true});
    window.addEventListener('mousedown', () => {                // press + ripple
      cur.classList.add('is-down','is-click');
      setTimeout(() => cur.classList.remove('is-click'), 560);
    });
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', () => cur.classList.remove('is-down'));
    document.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => { cur.style.opacity = '0'; });
    document.addEventListener('mouseenter', () => { cur.style.opacity = '1'; });
    const CLICK = 'a[href],button,[role="button"],[data-cursor],.ws-card,.wsec-row,summary,label[for],.thread-label,.mm-item,.side-dots a,.mm-toggle';
    const TEXT  = 'input,textarea,[contenteditable="true"]';
    document.addEventListener('mouseover', e => {
      const el = e.target;
      if (el.closest && el.closest(TEXT)){ cur.classList.add('is-text'); cur.classList.remove('is-hand','is-view','has-label'); return; }
      cur.classList.remove('is-text');
      const c = el.closest && el.closest(CLICK);
      if (c){
        const dc = c.closest('[data-cursor]');
        const isView = !!(dc && dc.dataset.cursor === 'view');   // portfolio cards
        cur.classList.toggle('is-view', isView);
        cur.classList.toggle('is-hand', !isView);
        cur.classList.remove('has-label');
      } else { cur.classList.remove('is-hand','is-view','has-label'); }
    }, {passive:true});
  }catch(err){ /* on any failure, native cursor remains */ }
})();
</script>
</body>

</html>
